- **perception-first-design**: Perception-First Design (PFD) — psychology-backed design framework. Auto-activates on design, marketing, copywriting, and communication tasks. Four commands: /perception-first-design:solve (derive a solution from cognitive constraints), /perception-first-design:evaluate (corpus-backed audit of an existing artifact), /perception-first-design:analyze (descriptive cascade for hypotheticals and mechanism questions), /perception-first-design:all (run all three modes on the same input). Mode auto-detection routes bare 'pfd this' invocations to the right mode based on input shape. ([skovalik/perception-first-design](https://github.com/skovalik/perception-first-design) · ★120 · 1 skill, 4 commands)

- **perf**: Structured performance investigation with baselines, profiling, and evidence-backed decisions — run by AI agents inside your editor. `/perf` enforces a rigorous 10-phase methodology derived from recorded real investigation sessions: establish a baseline, identify the breaking point, generate hypotheses, profile, run controlled experiments, measure, document. No random micro-optimizations; every change is baselined, isolated, measured, and either kept or reverted with evidence. Designed to keep the agent honest in a domain where intuition is usually wrong. ([agent-sh/perf](https://github.com/agent-sh/perf) · ★2 · 8 skills, 1 command, 6 agents)
